{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a87884da318b3013893b725d047b53702} 
\+\#define IMB\+\_\+\+GCM\+\_\+\+IV\+\_\+\+DATA\+\_\+\+LEN~(12)}

IV data is limited to 16 bytes as follows: 12 bytes is provided by an application -\/ pre-\/counter block j0: 4 byte salt (from Security Association) concatenated with 8 byte Initialization Vector (from IPSec ESP Payload). 4 byte value 0x00000001 is padded automatically by the library -\/ there is no need to add these 4 bytes on application side anymore. \Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a270311268c29ce07cffa688042b1855a}\index{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}!IMB\_GCM\_MAX\_LEN@{IMB\_GCM\_MAX\_LEN}}

\+\#define IMB\+\_\+\+GCM\+\_\+\+MAX\+\_\+\+LEN~UINT64\+\_\+C((((1ULL $<$$<$ 39) -\/ 256) /\+ 8) -\/ 1)}

\label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_doc-typedef-members}
\Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_doc-typedef-members}
\doxysubsection{Typedef Documentation}
\Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_aed2387ba36d72c3d148fcb9d2802ccf5}\index{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}!IMB\_JOB@{IMB\_JOB}}
\index{IMB\_JOB@{IMB\_JOB}!intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{IMB\_JOB}{IMB\_JOB}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_aed2387ba36d72c3d148fcb9d2802ccf5} 
typedef struct IMB\+\_\+\+JOB IMB\+\_\+\+JOB}

Job structure.

For AES, enc\+\_\+keys and dec\+\_\+keys are expected to point to expanded keys structure.
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item AES-\/\+CTR, AES-\/\+ECB and AES-\/\+CCM, only enc\+\_\+keys is used
\item DOCSIS (AES-\/\+CBC + AES-\/\+CFB), both pointers are used enc\+\_\+keys has to be set always for the partial block
\end{DoxyItemize}

For AES-\/\+GCM, AES-\/\+GCM-\/\+SGL and SM4-\/\+GCM, enc\+\_\+keys and dec\+\_\+keys are expected to point to the same key structure.
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item enc\+\_\+keys is used for encrypt direction and dec\+\_\+keys for decrypt direction
\end{DoxyItemize}

For DES, enc\+\_\+keys and dec\+\_\+keys are expected to point to DES key schedule.
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item same key schedule used for enc and dec operations
\end{DoxyItemize}

For 3DES, enc\+\_\+keys and dec\+\_\+keys are expected to point to an array of 3 pointers for the corresponding 3 key schedules.
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item same key schedule used for enc and dec operations
\end{DoxyItemize}

Cipher offset only applies to src pointer, not dst pointer. In case of an in-\/place operation, dst pointer needs to point at src + cipher\+\_\+start\+\_\+src\+\_\+offset\+\_\+in\+\_\+bytes. \Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}\index{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}!IMB\_MGR@{IMB\_MGR}}

Optional parameters for \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ac0b5fae08828f115f40479c942b9b1c3}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+dsa\+\_\+keypair()}. A NULL {\itshape params} pointer selects the common-\/case default: fresh-\/random key generation. Note that passing a bare {\ttfamily = \{ 0 \}} structure instead is not equivalent, as its {\itshape size} field would be rejected -\/ see {\itshape size} below.

Always initialize instances of this structure with \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a1c5fd3bda1ce2b8574ddd93879a430dc}{IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+\+KEYGEN\+\_\+\+PARAMS\+\_\+\+INIT()} (or otherwise ensure {\itshape size} is set to sizeof(\+IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+\+KEYGEN\+\_\+\+PARAMS)) -\/ see {\itshape size} below. \Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a480b48e9d368c098c99356145165e9ab}\index{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}!IMB\_ML\_DSA\_SIGN\_PARAMS@{IMB\_ML\_DSA\_SIGN\_PARAMS}}

typedef struct IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\+KEYGEN\+\_\+\+PARAMS IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\+KEYGEN\+\_\+\+PARAMS}

Optional parameters for \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a2873c501ebcee72b3acbc9c00d54003e}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+keypair()}. A NULL {\itshape params} pointer selects the common-\/case default: fresh-\/random key generation. Note that passing a bare {\ttfamily = \{ 0 \}} structure instead is not equivalent, as its {\itshape size} field would be rejected -\/ see {\itshape size} below.

Always initialize instances of this structure with \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a08f3ea3b7ea131f4310e57c7621ff80b}{IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\+KEYGEN\+\_\+\+PARAMS\+\_\+\+INIT()} (or otherwise ensure {\itshape size} is set to sizeof(\+IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\+KEYGEN\+\_\+\+PARAMS)) -\/ see {\itshape size} below. \Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_affa0d92b5f24f394d30ecf2680647b64}\index{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}!imb\_self\_test\_cb\_t@{imb\_self\_test\_cb\_t}}

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The library does not track key/\+\+IV (nonce) usage. For counter based and AEAD modes (CTR, GCM, CCM, Cha\+Cha20, Cha\+Cha20-\/\+Poly1305, SM4-\/\+CTR, SM4-\/\+GCM and the 3GPP algorithms) reusing an IV with the same key is a critical security failure and the application is responsible for guaranteeing uniqueness. See SECURITY.\+md "{}\+Key/\+\+IV (\+Nonce) Pair       Uniqueness"{}. 
\end{DoxyNote}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ab8dc617feb010b0f4cd74fd9e1eabb5e}

Allocates memory for multi-\/buffer manager instance. 

For binary compatibility between library versions it is recommended to use this API.


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em flags} & multi-\/buffer manager flags IMB\+\_\+\+FLAG\+\_\+\+SHANI\+\_\+\+OFF -\/ disable use (and detection) of SHA extensions, currently SHANI is only available for SSE IMB\+\_\+\+FLAG\+\_\+\+GFNI\+\_\+\+OFF -\/ disable use (and detection) of Galois Field extensions.\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}

\end{DoxyReturn}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em NULL} & on allocation error \\
\hline

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a189e7a2174fc137286073ad2d23d5884} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} int des\+\_\+cfb\+\_\+one (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{out}{, }\item[{const void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{in}{, }\item[{const uint64\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{iv}{, }\item[{const uint64\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ks}{, }\item[{const int}]{len}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



DES-\/\+CFB Encrypt/\+\+Decrypt a partial block (less than one block). 

Processes only one buffer at a time. Designed to manage partial blocks of DOCSIS 3.\+1 SEC BPI.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em out} & Plain/\+cipher text output \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em in} & Plain/\+cipher text input \\
\hline

Retrieves minimum burst size for good performance on AEAD algorithms. 

Depending on the architecture used, this function returns the minimum burst size to be used for good performance on the AEAD burst API. The output burst size can be 1 (in case of a synchronous single-\/buffer implementation or 0 if the algorithm is not supported by the API).

\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}

\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em iv} & Pointer to 12 byte IV structure Internally, the library concatenates 0x00000001 to the IV. Must be unique for every message encrypted with the same key (SP 800-\/38D section 8); uniqueness is not checked \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em aad} & Additional Authentication Data (AAD) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em aad\+\_\+len} & Length of AAD in bytes \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em auth\+\_\+tag} & Authenticated Tag output \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em auth\+\_\+tag\+\_\+len} & Authenticated Tag Length in bytes (must be a multiple of 4 bytes). Valid values are 16 (most likely), 12 or 8\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The library computes the authentication tag but does NOT verify it. The caller MUST compare {\itshape auth\+\_\+tag} against the tag received with the message using a constant-\/time comparison (not memcmp()) and MUST discard the plaintext if they differ. 
\end{DoxyNote}

AES-\/\+GCM-\/256 Decryption. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em key\+\_\+data} & GCM expanded key data \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em context\+\_\+data} & GCM operation context data \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em out} & Plain text output. Decrypt in-\/place is allowed \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em in} & Cipher text input \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em len} & Length of data in bytes for decryption \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em iv} & Pointer to 12 byte IV structure Internally, the library concatenates 0x00000001 to the IV. Must be unique for every message encrypted with the same key (SP 800-\/38D section 8); uniqueness is not checked \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em aad} & Additional Authentication Data (AAD) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em aad\+\_\+len} & Length of AAD in bytes \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em auth\+\_\+tag} & Authenticated Tag output \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em auth\+\_\+tag\+\_\+len} & Authenticated Tag Length in bytes (must be a multiple of 4 bytes). Valid values are 16 (most likely), 12 or 8\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The library computes the authentication tag but does NOT verify it. The caller MUST compare {\itshape auth\+\_\+tag} against the tag received with the message using a constant-\/time comparison (not memcmp()) and MUST discard the plaintext if they differ. 
\end{DoxyNote}

Finalize Cha\+Cha20-\/\+Poly1305 Decryption and output the authentication tag. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em ctx} & Cha\+Cha20-\/\+Poly1305 operation context data \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em tag} & Authenticated Tag output \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em tagl} & Authenticated Tag Length in bytes (must be 16)\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The library computes the authentication tag but does NOT verify it. The caller MUST compare {\itshape tag} against the tag received with the message using a constant-\/time comparison (not memcmp()) and MUST discard the plaintext if they differ. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}

\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} int imb\+\_\+cipher\+\_\+burst\+\_\+get\+\_\+size (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{mb\+\_\+mgr}{, }\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a3be1dfb4cbd4bd13dfdb2cf881f8334c}{IMB\+\_\+\+CIPHER\+\_\+\+MODE}}}]{cipher\+\_\+mode}{, }\item[{unsigned \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{out\+\_\+burst\+\_\+size}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Retrieves minimum burst size for good performance on cipher algorithms. 

Depending on the architecture used, this function returns the minimum burst size to be used for good performance on the cipher-\/only burst API. The output burst size can be 1 (in case of a synchronous single-\/buffer implementation or 0 if the algorithm is not supported by the API).


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em mb\+\_\+mgr} & pointer to IMB MGR structure \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em cipher\+\_\+mode} & cipher mode \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em out\+\_\+burst\+\_\+size} & pointer to store min burst size\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
operation status. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em 0} & success \\
\hline
{\em IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+CIPHER\+\_\+\+MODE} & not supported {\itshape algo} \\
\hline

\Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a79f60b2bffbe91dac3e4f8b486e7ab10}\index{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}!imb\_get\_errno@{imb\_get\_errno}}
\index{imb\_get\_errno@{imb\_get\_errno}!intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{imb\_get\_errno()}{imb\_get\_errno()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a79f60b2bffbe91dac3e4f8b486e7ab10} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} int imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+errno (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{mb\+\_\+mgr}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



API to get error status. 

Error status is kept per \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}; when {\itshape mb\+\_\+mgr} is not NULL and its status is set, that value is returned. Since an \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR} is owned by a single thread this makes the status thread specific. When {\itshape mb\+\_\+mgr} is NULL, or the manager has no error recorded, a process wide status is returned instead. That fallback is shared by all threads and can be overwritten by another thread\textquotesingle{}s API call, so it is only reliable for APIs that do not take an \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR} (e.\+g. \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ab8dc617feb010b0f4cd74fd9e1eabb5e}{alloc\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr()}). Always pass the manager used for the failing call when one exists.


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em mb\+\_\+mgr} & Pointer to multi-\/buffer manager (may be NULL)\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em Integer} & error type \\
\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

\Hypertarget{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_aab046807f5d2e70eec0291bb847b39a4}\index{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}!imb\_get\_next\_job@{imb\_get\_next\_job}}

\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structIMB__JOB}{IMB\+\_\+\+JOB}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+next\+\_\+job (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{state}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Get next available job. 

get\+\_\+next\+\_\+job() returns a job object. This must be filled in and returned via submit\+\_\+job() before get\+\_\+next\+\_\+job() is called again. After submit\+\_\+job() is called, one should call get\+\_\+completed\+\_\+job() at least once (and preferably until it returns NULL). get\+\_\+completed\+\_\+job() and flush\+\_\+job() returns a job object. This job object ceases to be usable at the next call to get\+\_\+next\+\_\+job().


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em state} & Pointer to initialized \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR} structure\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
Pointer to next free \doxylink{structIMB__JOB}{IMB\+\_\+\+JOB} in the queue 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} int imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+ooo\+\_\+mgr (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{state}{, }\item[{const int}]{index}{, }\item[{void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ooo\+\_\+ptr}{, }\item[{size\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ooo\+\_\+size}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ooo\+\_\+name}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Get information about an out-\/of-\/order (OOO) manager for testing. 

This API allows test applications to access individual OOO manager structures for memory scanning to detect sensitive data leaks.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em state} & Pointer to \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR} structure \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em index} & Index of OOO manager (-\/1 for \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}, then 0 to count-\/1) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em ooo\+\_\+ptr} & Pointer to OOO manager structure (can be NULL if not initialized) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em ooo\+\_\+size} & Size of OOO manager in bytes (can be NULL) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em ooo\+\_\+name} & Name of OOO manager as NULL-\/terminated string (can be NULL)\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
0 on success, -\/1 if index is out of bounds
\end{DoxyReturn}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
This API is for TESTING/\+\+VALIDATION ONLY and should not be used in production code. 

If the OOO manager at the given index is NULL (not initialized), ooo\+\_\+ptr will be NULL but the function will still return 0 (success) as long as index is valid. 
\end{DoxyNote}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a0f415a67fcedcb31c2cec879655c3b86} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} int imb\+\_\+hash\+\_\+burst\+\_\+get\+\_\+size (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{mb\+\_\+mgr}{, }\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a83132be2c4a6bf0453550bd1ae720006}{IMB\+\_\+\+HASH\+\_\+\+ALG}}}]{algo}{, }\item[{unsigned \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{out\+\_\+burst\+\_\+size}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Retrieves minimum burst size for good performance on hash algorithms. 

Depending on the architecture used, this function returns the minimum burst size to be used for good performance on the hash-\/only burst API. Note that this will not return a value for best performance, but the minimum needed to start maximizing the CPU core (i.\+e. enough buffers to utilize efficiently the CPU core resources, taking into account that when buffers have different sizes, a higher burst size is recommended).

The output burst size may also be 1 (in case of a synchronous single-\/buffer implementation or 0 if the algorithm is not supported by the API).


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em mb\+\_\+mgr} & pointer to IMB MGR structure \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em algo} & hash algorithm \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em out\+\_\+burst\+\_\+size} & pointer to store min burst size\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
operation status. 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em 0} & success \\
\hline
{\em \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_aa12c6dbc4b212887ef528c215f513e0ba8ac2802661bd9928d0c766129db2a814}{IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+HASH\+\_\+\+ALGO}} & not supported {\itshape algo} \\
\hline
{\em \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_aa12c6dbc4b212887ef528c215f513e0ba9ba69cc32a2d17e3e7b4d4c982f26abd}{IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+NULL\+\_\+\+MBMGR}} & invalid {\itshape mb\+\_\+mgr} pointer \\

\hline
\end{DoxyRetVals}

Generate an ML-\/\+DSA key pair (FIPS 204 Key\+Gen) and bind it to {\itshape self}, replacing any previously bound key. The generated key carries both private and public components, so {\itshape self} may be used with both the sign and verify functions immediately afterwards. 


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em self} & ML-\/\+DSA context \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em pk} & Encoded public key buffer (fixed size: variant PUBKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES, e.\+g. IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+65\+\_\+\+PUBKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES for IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+65 -\/ see the IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\+\_\+\+PUBKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES macros above) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em pk\+\_\+len} & Capacity of {\itshape pk} in bytes, must be at least the variant PUBKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em sk} & Encoded private key buffer (fixed size: variant PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES, e.\+g. IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+65\+\_\+\+PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES for IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+65 -\/ see the IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\+\_\+\+PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES macros above) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em sk\+\_\+len} & Capacity of {\itshape sk} in bytes, must be at least the variant PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em params} & Optional key generation parameters, or NULL for fresh-\/random key generation\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\end{DoxyReturn}

Allocate and initialize an ML-\/\+DSA context for a given parameter set. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The context keeps a reference to {\itshape mgr} and checks its self-\/test state on every operation, so {\itshape mgr} must remain valid (not freed) until the context is released with \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ae125d85a0e86ef21f114ce042b53b913}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+dsa\+\_\+free()}.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{imb\_ml\_dsa\_set\_privkey()}{imb\_ml\_dsa\_set\_privkey()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a9e6ef3571579516f867aec3355d813e2} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} int imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+dsa\+\_\+set\+\_\+privkey (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a7a374acfcaff5ccdc6ce61ec91d2b065}{IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+DSA}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{self}{, }\item[{const void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{sk}{, }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{sk\+\_\+len}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Bind an encoded ML-\/\+DSA private key to the context, replacing any previously bound key. The key is decoded and fully validated (its public component is re-\/derived and the embedded consistency hash checked) once here; every subsequent sign/\+verify call on {\itshape self} reuses the cached, decoded key instead of repeating that work. The bound key carries both private and public components, so it may also be used with the verify functions.

\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em sk\+\_\+len} & Size of {\itshape sk} in bytes, must match the variant PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Operation status 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em 0} & success \\

Decapsulate, recovering the shared secret from a ciphertext. Requires a decapsulation key to have been bound to {\itshape self} via \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a2873c501ebcee72b3acbc9c00d54003e}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+keypair()} or \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a47a0b0db41b10e3c31a3f87bacc1f482}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+set\+\_\+privkey()}. 

Per FIPS 203, decapsulation never signals a cryptographic failure for a content-\/invalid (but correctly sized) ciphertext: the "{}implicit rejection"{} mechanism always returns a (pseudorandom, but deterministic per key and ciphertext) shared secret in that case. Only a ciphertext whose length does not match the bound parameter set is rejected as an error (FIPS 203 Section 7.\+3 mandates this length check on every call).


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em self} & ML-\/\+KEM context with a bound decapsulation key \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em shared\+\_\+secret} & Shared secret buffer (IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\+SHARED\+\_\+\+SECRET\+\_\+\+BYTES) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ss\+\_\+len} & Capacity of {\itshape shared\+\_\+secret} in bytes, must be at least IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\+SHARED\+\_\+\+SECRET\+\_\+\+BYTES \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ct} & Ciphertext buffer \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ct\+\_\+len} & Ciphertext length in bytes (must equal the bound parameter set\textquotesingle{}s CIPHERTEXT\+\_\+\+BYTES) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em params} & Reserved for future use; pass NULL\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Operation status 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em 0} & success \\
\hline

Encapsulate, producing a ciphertext and shared secret. Requires an encapsulation key to have been bound to {\itshape self} via \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a2873c501ebcee72b3acbc9c00d54003e}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+keypair()}, \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a47a0b0db41b10e3c31a3f87bacc1f482}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+set\+\_\+privkey()} (private keys carry the public component too) or \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a56eff8ebd7eb32505e331982bc208a81}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+set\+\_\+pubkey()}.

\hline

Generate an ML-\/\+KEM key pair (FIPS 203 Key\+Gen) and bind it to {\itshape self}, replacing any previously bound key. The generated key carries both private and public components, so {\itshape self} may be used with both the encapsulate and decapsulate functions immediately afterwards.

IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+768\+\_\+\+PUBKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES for IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+768 -\/ see the IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\+\_\+\+PUBKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES macros above) \\

\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em dk} & Encoded decapsulation key buffer (fixed size: variant PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES, e.\+g. IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+768\+\_\+\+PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES for IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+768 -\/ see the IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\+\_\+\+PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES macros above) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em dk\+\_\+len} & Capacity of {\itshape dk} in bytes, must be at least the variant PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES \\

Allocate and initialize an ML-\/\+KEM context for a parameter set. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The context keeps a reference to {\itshape mgr} and checks its self-\/test state on every operation, so {\itshape mgr} must remain valid (not freed) until the context is released with \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ac4bfccdf0f4c78e73f56176fcec9ff3e}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+free()}.
\end{DoxyNote}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a47a0b0db41b10e3c31a3f87bacc1f482} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} int imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+set\+\_\+privkey (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a7aacfe9c525be8c446795eac4f7d15b5}{IMB\+\_\+\+ML\+\_\+\+KEM}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{self}{, }\item[{const void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{dk}{, }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{dk\+\_\+len}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Bind an encoded ML-\/\+KEM decapsulation (private) key to the context, replacing any previously bound key. The key is decoded and fully validated (its public component is re-\/derived and the embedded consistency hash checked -\/ FIPS 203 Section 7.\+3 decapsulation key check) once here; every subsequent decapsulate call on {\itshape self} reuses the cached, decoded key instead of repeating that work. The bound key carries both private and public components, so it may also be used with the encapsulate functions.

\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em dk} & Encoded decapsulation key (variant PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES) \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em dk\+\_\+len} & Size of {\itshape dk} in bytes, must match the variant PRIVKEY\+\_\+\+BYTES \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
Operation status 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\begin{DoxyRetVals}{Return values}
{\em 0} & success \\

Bind an encoded ML-\/\+KEM encapsulation (public) key to the context, replacing any previously bound key. The key is decoded once here (its encoded length and coefficient ranges are checked -\/ FIPS 203 Section 7.\+2 encapsulation key check); every subsequent encapsulate call on {\itshape self} reuses the cached, decoded key instead of repeating that work. Initializes \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR} pointers to out-\/of-\/order managers with use of externally allocated memory.

init\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr\+\_\+\+XXX() must be called after this function call, whereas XXX is the desired architecture.

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
If the manager is in the fail-\/closed self-\/test error state (see \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a2cbbd032835837fd0aa83b04f5337638}{init\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr\+\_\+auto()}) and {\itshape reset\+\_\+mgr} is 0, that state is preserved: the API\textquotesingle{}s remain disabled until a subsequent successful init\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr\+\_\+\+XXX() call.
\end{DoxyNote}

\hline

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{imb\_set\_session()}{imb\_set\_session()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ada304337e78ef5a462c97c1f8151ef67} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} uint32\+\_\+t imb\+\_\+set\+\_\+session (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{state}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{structIMB__JOB}{IMB\+\_\+\+JOB}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{job}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Sets up suite\+\_\+id and session\+\_\+id fields for selected cipher suite in provided {\itshape job} structure. 

This is mandatory operation for BURST API as suite\+\_\+id is used to speed up job dispatch process. This operation is optional but helpful for JOB API use case.

\textquotesingle{}session\+\_\+id\textquotesingle{} field is for application use to optimize job set up process. If JOB structure provided by library for a new operation has same session ID as required for the next operation then only message pointers and sizes need to be set up by the application. All other session fields are guaranteed to be unmodified by the library:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item cipher mode
\item cipher direction
\item hash algorithm
\item key size
\item encrypt \& decrypt key pointers
\item suite\+\_\+id If allocated JOB structure contains different session ID then all required session and crypto operation fields need to be set up.
\end{DoxyItemize}

In connection oriented applications, a template filled-\/in job structure can be cached within connection structure and reused in submit operations.

For given set of parameters: cipher mode, cipher key size, cipher direction and authentication mode, suite\+\_\+id field is the same.

Submit multiple jobs to be processed after validating. 

Prior to submission, {\itshape \+\_\+jobs} need to be initialized with correct crypto job parameters and followed with a call to \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ada304337e78ef5a462c97c1f8151ef67}{imb\+\_\+set\+\_\+session()}.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em state} & Pointer to initialized \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR} structure \\
\hline

\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em jobs} & In: List of pointers to jobs for submission Out: List of pointers to completed jobs\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\end{DoxySeeAlso}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
Number of completed jobs or zero on error. If zero, \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a79f60b2bffbe91dac3e4f8b486e7ab10}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+errno()} can be used to check for potential error conditions and \+\_\+jobs\+[0] contains pointer to invalid job 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\index{init\_mb\_mgr\_auto@{init\_mb\_mgr\_auto}!intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h@{intel-\/ipsec-\/mb.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{init\_mb\_mgr\_auto()}{init\_mb\_mgr\_auto()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a2cbbd032835837fd0aa83b04f5337638} 
\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a986643689f56109161597f67eb9d7b40}{IMB\+\_\+\+DLL\+\_\+\+EXPORT}} void init\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr\+\_\+auto (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a6d466496b4adfea3f9bc0881f11fe551}{IMB\+\_\+\+MGR}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{state}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ad138a5bf5508a0be5d3d61f018d5ebb6}{IMB\+\_\+\+ARCH}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{arch}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Automatically initialize most performant Multi-\/buffer manager based on CPU features. 

Manager initialization and the power-\/up self-\/test.

Every init\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}() call runs the library self-\/test (see IMB\+\_\+\+FEATURE\+\_\+\+SELF\+\_\+\+TEST and IMB\+\_\+\+FEATURE\+\_\+\+SELF\+\_\+\+TEST\+\_\+\+PASS). If the test fails the manager enters a fail-\/closed error state:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a79f60b2bffbe91dac3e4f8b486e7ab10}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+errno()} returns IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+SELFTEST and IMB\+\_\+\+FEATURE\+\_\+\+SELF\+\_\+\+TEST\+\_\+\+PASS is cleared in the feature flags;
\item every job, burst and direct API on that manager is disabled: calls return NULL / 0 / -\/1 as appropriate, do not write to any output buffer and set IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+SELFTEST;
\item \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ad3fdcf3dd90efcc85c4222f5ff58c48e}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+new()} and \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a3a2c42ec0cc48d52fdc32d36d937bbe6}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+dsa\+\_\+new()} return IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+SELFTEST, and every operation on ML-\/\+KEM/\+\+ML-\/\+DSA contexts created earlier with that manager (other than \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ac4bfccdf0f4c78e73f56176fcec9ff3e}{imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+kem\+\_\+free()}/\+imb\+\_\+ml\+\_\+dsa\+\_\+free()) returns IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+SELFTEST;
\item \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a79f60b2bffbe91dac3e4f8b486e7ab10}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+errno()}, \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a61f664448a862fbb02e728ce518ff8e4}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+strerror()}, \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_ae817018bc0c7bbd3cad0147952c8576e}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+features()}, \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a8bf5a6c43278a8ed16850a46707db863}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+flags()}, \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a474ce25401eac36333b69f27c669849c}{imb\+\_\+self\+\_\+test\+\_\+set\+\_\+cb()}/\+imb\+\_\+self\+\_\+test\+\_\+get\+\_\+cb() and \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a238b96cc36c0dc64798a1244b94add8b}{free\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr()} remain functional. The application should check \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a79f60b2bffbe91dac3e4f8b486e7ab10}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+errno()} after initialization. A subsequent successful init\+\_\+mb\+\_\+mgr\+\_\+\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}() call on the same manager restores normal operation. 
\end{DoxyItemize}


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em state} & Pointer to MB\+\_\+\+MGR struct \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em arch} & Pointer to arch enum to be set (can be NULL)\\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
On self-\/test failure the manager is left in the fail-\/closed state described above and \doxylink{intel-ipsec-mb_8h_a79f60b2bffbe91dac3e4f8b486e7ab10}{imb\+\_\+get\+\_\+errno()} returns IMB\+\_\+\+ERR\+\_\+\+SELFTEST. 
\end{DoxyNote}
